# A/R and A/P Report Sorting, Export and Location Filter: setup file for Claude sessions

This file is for a Claude session. A manual tester never needs it: each test case is complete on its own. It holds no environment address, credentials, cookies or session data. Generated by `authoring/render_setup.py` from the case sources; regenerate it whenever a case changes.

## 1. Header and sources
- Feature: A/R and A/P Report Sorting, Export and Location Filter (epic SV-11315; stories SV-11531 to SV-11539). TestRail group 90418, sections in `sections.json`. 357 cases: created 22 Oct 2026; the full pass against PRD version 9 and Chris Ward's answers (26 Oct 2026, `v9-pass-2026-10-26/`) is drafted here and not yet written to TestRail.
- PRD: PRD "A/R and A/P Report Sorting, Export and Location Filter", Confluence 947650561, version 9 (23 Oct 2026, Chris Ward); saved `sources/CONFLUENCE-947650561-v9-2026-10-26.md` (pandoc from the page's HTML, `sources/CONFLUENCE-947650561-v9-2026-10-26.html`), read in full 26 Oct 2026. Version 5 (`sources/CONFLUENCE-947650561-v5-2026-10-22.md`) is kept for the record; `sources/PRD-DIFF-v5-to-v9.json` lists the requirement lines that changed.
- PO answers: Chris Ward's answers to the A/R and A/P Report Sorting question sheet (questions 1 to 16, Google Sheet 11_3z_TpKkOL-MiAWqxrj1jlHP1rn9QXu_h3uCR0SjVA, answered 23 Oct 2026); saved `questions-2026-10-22/chris-answers-2026-10-26.json`. PRD version 9 carries them; a case quotes an answer only where PRD version 9 is silent.
- Jira: every ticket under the epic read 22 Oct 2026, `sources/JIRA-READING-2026-10-22.md` (differences from the PRD listed there).
- Design: https://claude.ai/artifact/BRYCdjKwGRSgGPC9xxkgF4 — read in full 22 Oct 2026, saved in `design-2026-10-22/` (7 boards, 45 figures; the 43 screenshots in `design-2026-10-22/images/`, every word transcribed in `TRANSCRIPTS.md`). Each recipe below names the pictures its case compares with. Design-vs-PRD differences: `DESIGN-DIVERGENCE-REGISTER.json`.
- Tech plan: "A/R and A/P Report Sorting, Export and Location Filter — Technical Implementation Plan" (2026-10-21, written against PRD v4), uploaded by the QA lead 22 Oct 2026, saved `sources/Tech-Plan-AR-AP-2026-10-22-upload.md`; read in full and every case checked against it (`tech-plan-pass-2026-10-22/`). Its quotes sit under their own "From:" line in the cases that use them.
- No build yet. Every recipe below is UNVERIFIED until the build verification session runs it.
- At session start, ask the user for the site to use and how to sign in as each slot below. Never write a secret into this file.

## 3. Flags and settings
- No feature setting: the PRD says nobody gains or loses access and no setup is needed.
- Browser: desktop, window 1440 px wide at 100% zoom unless the case names 1536, 1280, 1200 or 900 px; light theme unless the case says dark.
- Every report is opened from the Reports menu so nothing is remembered from an earlier visit. On screen (design pictures): Reports in the top menu; the left menu lists "A/R Aging Summary", "A/R Aging Detail", "A/R Aging Collection" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE and "A/P Aging Summary", "A/P Aging Detail", "A/P Unpaid Invoices" under ACCOUNTS PAYABLE (page title "A/P Unpaid Invoices Report"); Sales By Customer and Work In Progress under PERFORMANCE, Inventory Value under PARTS.
- Design picture cases (D-50 to D-75 and D-81; D-76 to D-80 were deleted on 26 Oct 2026): open the named picture from `design-2026-10-22/images/` (in TestRail, attached to the case) at 100% zoom beside the browser.
- Comparison tabs: design cases open Sales By Customer, Inventory Value or Work In Progress in a second tab, as the case says.
- Test-site facts from the tech plan (21 Oct 2026): (1) on staging and QA the reports read the main database ("Staging/QA reader = writer"), so a change shows on reload at once; production reads a copy about a second behind. (2) The PDF generator is "not configured on every environment": if every PDF fails with "We couldn't export this report. Please try again." while CSVs work, the site lacks it; record Blocked, not Failed. (3) A 2,000-row PDF takes about 30 seconds. (4) Enrolling the shared Admin at another location changes the Location chip of every other report; prefer a separate user for multi-location setups. (5) The Customer menu lists at most 50 customers until you search, from all the organization's customers; the Vendor menu lists all the organization's vendors and shows its search box only above 8 (PRD v9 S5-R13 says so too). (6) Every default role with report access also has the "View and Manage AP/AR Data" switch, and the default Technician role has no report access: a permission split needs a custom role. (7) The page address keeps the filters as asOfDate, customerIds / vendorIds, pastDue (1-30, 31-60, 61-90, 91-plus) and locationIds, each id a long code written as its own entry; a comma list is also read.

## 4. Shared procedures (UNVERIFIED click paths; no build yet)
| Procedure | What | How | Check |
|---|---|---|---|
| P-LOC | Locations | Two staging locations the Admin is enrolled at ({Location-A}, {Location-B}); a third ({Location-C}) and nine in all only where a case asks. Settings > Locations (UNVERIFIED path). A long-named location for the chip case. | The top-right location switcher lists them. |
| P-CUST | Customer | Customers > New Customer: the example name, a phone number and a credit term where the case names them (none where it says none). Every name carries ZZAUTOTEST and the case key. | The customer card shows the name, phone and credit term. |
| P-VENDOR | Vendor | Vendors > New Vendor with the example name. A vendor has no single location of its own: each bill or credit belongs to the location it is received or entered at (tech plan, 21 Oct 2026, Q2 and D13). | The vendor page shows it. |
| P-INV-WO | Work order invoice | Work Orders > Create Work Order for the customer at the named location; add a labor line or part to reach the amount; Finance tab > Create Invoice (close the payment dialog unpaid). | The invoice shows on A/R Detail with the amount. |
| P-INV-PS | Part sale invoice | Part Sales > New Part Sale for the customer at the named location; add a part to reach the amount; invoice it unpaid. | The invoice shows on A/R Detail. |
| P-PAY | Partial payment | On the invoice record the payment amount the case names; for a payment taken elsewhere, switch the active location first. | The open balance equals amount minus payment. |
| P-CM | Credit memo / deposit | Create an unused credit memo or a deposit for the customer at the named location, for the amount named. | It shows on A/R Detail as its own row. |
| P-BILL | Bill | Vendor Order Management: create a vendor order, receive it on a delivery at the named location and enter the bill with the amount; leave it unpaid. | The bill shows on A/P Detail. |
| P-VC | Vendor credit | From a vendor return made at the named location (vendor credit from a return), or a vendor credit entered without a return while signed in at the named location, for the amount named. | It shows on A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices, at that location. |
| P-AGE | Dates and due dates | UNVERIFIED: the aging cases need documents whose date and due date sit a set number of days before the as-of date (5, 10, 15, 20, 30, 31, 35, 40, 45, 60, 61, 90, 91, 100 days). If the build cannot back-date, use payment terms (Net 30 etc.) and existing records of the right age, or move the as-of date forward and adjust the expected days; record which in the run notes. Same-day cases (S1-10, S1-11): the document with the higher number must be invoiced (or, for a bill, entered) earlier in the day than the one with the lower number: create both work orders first (the numbers are given then), invoice the second one in the morning and the first one in the afternoon, both with the same invoice date. | The Past due column shows the expected days; for same-day cases, the invoice times are noted. |
| P-ROLE | Custom role and user | Settings > Roles: copy a role and set only the permissions the case names (Reports → Page Access always on unless the case says off); Settings > Staff: a user with that role, enrolled at the locations the case names. | Signing in as the user shows only what the case expects. |
| P-PRE-RELEASE | Before-the-release capture | For REL cases marked PRE_REL: before the release is installed, create the records, take the screenshots or exports named and keep them with their dates. | Files saved and dated. |
| P-OFFLINE | Lost connection | Switch the computer's Wi-Fi off (or unplug the network cable) after the report has loaded; switch it back on at the end. | The browser shows it is offline. |
| P-VOLUME | Large data | Paging and limit cases need many rows: use the QA organization (13,540 open A/P lines; 46 A/R Summary customers) or create ZZAUTOTEST customers or bills in bulk. Exact 2,000 / 2,001 PDF boundary (A/P Detail): on the day of the test, export the A/P Detail CSV with every vendor, count open lines per vendor by script, pick a set of vendors ({Vendors-A}) whose lines add up to exactly 2,000 (prefer few, large vendors; if no exact set exists, top up with ZZAUTOTEST bills for one vendor in the set), and create one ZZAUTOTEST vendor with exactly one open bill ({Vendor-B}); write both into the case's values. The 2,000-customer Summary boundary and the 50,000 / 50,001 CSV boundary cannot be reached on any test site and are checked by the developers (SV-11595, SV-11598). The Vendor menu without a search box (S5-39) needs a test organization with 8 vendors or fewer: UNVERIFIED whether one exists; ask the user, and if none can be made, mark the case Blocked "setup not done". | The counts in the case hold. |

## 5. One recipe per case: the placeholder values (the examples in the case are the values)
### SETUP-S1-01 — A/R Summary opens with customers in name order, A to Z
Section: Story 1 — Reports open in QuickBooks name order (TestRail section 90419). Reports: A/R Summary. Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions, exactly as the case states them:
- You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, with {Location-A} as your current location. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- Four customers have open invoices on today's date: {Customer-A}, {Customer-B}, {Customer-C} and {Customer-D}. Their names start with the same word and then differ by a number, a dash or capitals, as in the example. For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-01 10 Trucking, {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-01 2 Trucking, {Customer-C} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-01 A-1 Towing, {Customer-D} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-01 a1 towing.
- Starting screen: A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu.
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
Placeholders: {Customer-A}, {Customer-B}, {Customer-C}, {Customer-D}, {Location-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real document numbers here after setup.
Procedures: P-LOC, P-CUST, P-INV-WO.
Sources (verbatim text in section 9): S1-R1, S1-R2, S1-R7.
Design pictures: `design-2026-10-22/ar-ap-p7-a-r-summary-date-only.png` (I01, "A/R Summary: date only"), `design-2026-10-22/ar-ap-p7-a-r-summary-date-only.png` (I01, "A/R Aging Summary as it opens today"), `design-2026-10-22/ar-ap-p1-a-r-aging-summary-opens-a-to-z.png` (I03, "A/R Aging Summary opens A to Z").

### SETUP-S1-08 — A/R Detail lists the five buckets in fixed order and rows by name
Section: Story 1 — Reports open in QuickBooks name order (TestRail section 90419). Reports: A/R Detail. Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions, exactly as the case states them:
- You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, with {Location-A} as your current location. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- The report has open documents in all five aging buckets on today's date.
- In the "1-30 days past due" bucket, {Customer-A} has one open invoice, {Invoice-A}, and {Customer-B} has two, {Invoice-B} and {Invoice-C}. {Customer-B}'s name comes first alphabetically, and {Invoice-B}'s number is smaller than {Invoice-C}'s but has fewer digits. For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-08 Baker and {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST S1-08 Abbott; {Invoice-B} is S1-99 and {Invoice-C} is S1-100 (the app assigns the real numbers).
- Starting screen: A/R Detail (Reports, then "A/R Aging Detail" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu.
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
Placeholders: {Customer-A}, {Customer-B}, {Invoice-A}, {Invoice-B}, {Invoice-C}, {Location-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real document numbers here after setup.

- The report has open documents at both locations on today's date.
- Starting screen: A/R Collection (Reports, then "A/R Aging Collection" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu, with every group expanded (Expand all).
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

- {Customer-A} has an invoice of $1,000.00 that is not yet due, an invoice of $400.00 45 days past due, an invoice of $300.00 15 days past due and an unused credit memo of $150.00; {Customer-B} has an invoice of $500.00 45 days past due and an invoice of $250.00 100 days past due. Nothing else is open for the two on today's date. For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST DATA-23 Filtered One and {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST DATA-23 Filtered Two.
- Starting screen: A/R Summary (Reports, then "A/R Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS RECEIVABLE in the left menu) is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu, with {Customer-A} and {Customer-B} ticked in the Customer filter.
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
Placeholders: {Customer-A}, {Customer-B}, {Location-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real document numbers here after setup.

### SETUP-D-16b — Design: A/P Unpaid Invoices Open Balance column and Totals row look native
Section: Design — how it looks (TestRail section 90430). Reports: A/P Unpaid Invoices. Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions, exactly as the case states them:
- You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, with {Location-A} as your current location. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- The organization has open documents in these reports at two locations, {Location-A} and {Location-B}, and you are enrolled at both; {Location-A}'s name comes first in A to Z order. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919 and {Location-B} is Staging Lethbridge - 4310.
- Vendor {Vendor-A} has a long name (more than 40 characters) and open documents at both locations on today's date, including a vendor credit (its Transaction Type is the longest). For example: {Vendor-A} is ZZAUTOTEST D-16 Northern Alberta Heavy Equipment and Trailer Repair.
- Starting screen: A/P Unpaid Invoices (Reports, then "A/P Unpaid Invoices" under ACCOUNTS PAYABLE in the left menu; the page title reads "A/P Unpaid Invoices Report") is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu in light theme at 100% zoom, with every group expanded (Expand all).
- Sales By Customer (Reports, then "Sales By Customer" under PERFORMANCE in the left menu) is open in a second tab of the same window, for comparison.
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".
Placeholders: {Location-A}, {Location-B}, {Vendor-A}. Values: the "For example" lines above; write the real document numbers here after setup.
Procedures: P-LOC, P-CUST, P-VENDOR, P-INV-WO, P-PAY, P-VC.
Sources (verbatim text in section 9): KD-REUSE, S8-R3, S8-R4.

### SETUP-D-73 — Design: the Vendor menu matches the design picture
Section: Design — how it looks (TestRail section 90430). Reports: A/P Summary. Marker: `AUTOMATION: HOLD - not yet build-verified (no build yet)`.
Preconditions, exactly as the case states them:
- You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role, with {Location-A} as your current location. For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
- The organization has more than 8 vendors (the Vendor menu shows its search box only when there are more than 8 vendors). For example: the QA organization (437 vendors).
- Starting screen: A/P Summary (Reports, then "A/P Aging Summary" under ACCOUNTS PAYABLE in the left menu) is open in a desktop browser window 1440 px wide, as opened from the Reports menu in light theme at 100% zoom.
- Inventory Value (Reports, then "Inventory Value" under PARTS in the left menu) is open in a second tab of the same window, for comparison.
- The design picture "The native Vendor menu" (ar-ap-p7-the-native-vendor-menu.png) is attached to this case and open beside the browser at 100% zoom; compare the layout, labels and styles, not the names and amounts, which are only examples.
- If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

## 7. Dependencies and shared fixtures
- Every case owns its records: the example names carry ZZAUTOTEST and the case key. Paging, volume and limit cases share the QA organization's existing data (read only).
- Release-day cases (PRE_REL marker) need their records and captures made before the release is installed on the test site.

## 8. Reset and cleanup
- Leave ZZAUTOTEST records in place. Switch the network back on after offline cases. Return the active location to {Location-A} after a switch-location case. Set the theme back to light.

## 9. Traceability — every source sentence the cases quote (PRD version 9 unless marked otherwise)
- **S1-E1**: "A customer with no open documents on the as-of date does not appear, as today."
- **S1-N1**: "If the user lacks 'Reports → Page Access', the reports are not available, as today."
- **S1-R1**: "A/R Summary opens with customers in name order, A to Z; A/P Summary opens with vendors in name order, A to Z."
- **S1-R2**: "Name order compares names ignoring capitals, in plain character order: digits and punctuation compare as characters, so "10 Trucking" comes before "2 Trucking" and "A-1 Towing" before "a1 towing". Names that start with a punctuation mark follow the same order: "#1 Diesel", "-1 Diesel", "10 Trucking", "@Home Towing"."
- **S1-R3**: "Accented letters sort with their base letter: "Émile's" sorts among the E names (é è ê ë sort as e, á à â ä ã å as a, ç as c, ñ as n, ö ó ò ô õ ø as o, ü ú ù û as u, í ì î ï as i, ý ÿ as y, ß as ss, æ as ae, œ as oe, in both cases)."
- **S1-R4**: "Two names that compare equal are ordered by the plain lower-cased name, then by the customer or vendor itself, so the same two customers always come out in the same order on screen, in the PDF and in the CSV. For example, "Emile's Towing" comes before "Émile's Towing": the accent folds for the name order (S1-R3), so the two tie, and in the plain lower-cased name the unaccented letter comes first."
- **S1-R5**: "A/R Detail and A/P Detail keep their aging buckets in this fixed order: "91 or more days past due", "61-90 days past due", "31-60 days past due", "1-30 days past due", "Current". Inside each bucket, rows open in customer or vendor name order (S1-R2 to S1-R4), ties by document number (S2-R4)."
- **S1-R6**: "A/R Collection groups by customer and A/P Unpaid Invoices by vendor, groups in name order (S1-R2 to S1-R4); inside each group, rows open oldest date first; rows on the same date, whatever the time of day they were created, go in document-number order (S2-R4)."
- **S1-R7**: "The column the report opened on is marked as sorted: the name column on the Summaries and Detail reports, the Date column on Collection and Unpaid Invoices."
- **S1-R8**: "A/R Summary, A/P Summary, A/R Collection and A/P Unpaid Invoices show 30 customers or vendors at a time, and the next 30 load as the user scrolls down, as today. There are no page buttons. A/R Detail and A/P Detail show each bucket's first 100 lines, then load more as the user scrolls (S4-R4). Paging never repeats or skips a customer, vendor or row, in any sort."
- **S2-E1**: "A column where every row has the same value (for example Location when each customer has one location) shows no visible change; the tie-break (S2-R4) still applies."
- **S2-N1**: "On A/P Unpaid Invoices, the Vendor Name header is not clickable, because every group holds a single vendor and sorting by it could never change the order."
- **S2-R1**: "Every column on all six reports sorts when its header is clicked, except A/P Unpaid Invoices' Vendor Name column (S2-N1). The columns are:"
- **S2-R2**: "The first click gives the useful order for the kind of data: text (names, Location, Transaction Type) A to Z; money columns largest first; dates oldest first; Past Due largest first; ID and No. smallest number first (S2-R4). The second click reverses it. The third click repeats the first; there is no unsorted state."
- **S2-R3**: "A header that is already the sorted column reverses on its first click."
- **S2-R4**: "ID and No. sort by number value: S1-99 before S1-100 before S1-101. The first click puts the smallest number first and the second click the largest first. The same number-aware order breaks ties in every sort, always smallest document number first: reversing a sort reverses the sorted column only, so tied rows keep that order in both directions. A date column compares the day only, never the time of day, so two rows on the same date are a tie."
- **S2-R4a**: "Column headings are in Title Case on all six reports, on screen, in the PDF and in the CSV, as on the other reports (Sales By Customer, Inventory Value, Parts Velocity): the A/R reports read "Transaction Type", "Customer Full Name", "Due Date", "Past Due" and "Open Balance", the same as the A/P reports."
- **S2-R5**: "On the grouped reports, a header click sorts the rows inside each group; the groups themselves never move (S1-R5, S1-R6)."
- **S2-R6**: "A blank value (for example a vendor credit on A/P Unpaid Invoices, which has no due date and no Past Due) sorts to the bottom of its group in both directions. On A/P Detail a vendor credit shows its own date as its due date, as today, so it is not blank there."
- **S2-R7**: "The sort is kept while the user stays on the report: changing the as-of date, loading more rows by scrolling, and changing the customer, vendor, Past due or Location filter keep it."
- **S2-R8**: "The sort is not remembered between visits: opening the report again starts from Story 1's order."
- **S2-R9**: "Re-sorting never changes which groups are expanded or collapsed (Story 4)."
- **S3-N1**: "Over 2,000 rows, the PDF is refused before anything is generated: no download, and the message "This report is too large for PDF. Export it as CSV, or narrow the filters, then try again." Exactly 2,000 rows is allowed."
- **S3-N2**: "Over 50,000 rows, the CSV is refused: no download, and the message "This report is too large to export. Choose an earlier as-of date, then try again." Exactly 50,000 rows is allowed."
- **S3-N3**: "A refused export shows exactly one message (S3-N1 or S3-N2), never a second generic error."
- **S3-R1**: "Each of the six reports has the native report Export menu (the ⋯ button at the right of the title row, labelled "Export report") with "Download (PDF)" and "Download (CSV)". A/R's former Print button is replaced by this menu. The A/P reports gain a PDF."
- **S3-R10**: "A successful export shows the native "Success" message, "Data exported successfully." An export with no rows downloads nothing and shows the native warning "Empty export", "Export didn't yield any results"."
- **S3-R2**: "The PDF downloads as a file."
- **S3-R3**: "The PDF and the CSV contain exactly the rows on screen for the chosen as-of date, customer or vendor and locations, in the on-screen sort, including a sort changed after an earlier export, without refreshing the page."
- **S3-R4**: "Each export states the as-of date the user picked: the PDF "As of Oct 15, 2026", the CSV "As of: Oct 15, 2026" (format: three-letter month, day, four-digit year)."
- **S3-R5**: "Each export states the locations under the as-of line: "Locations: All locations" when every location is ticked, otherwise "Locations: " followed by the ticked locations' names in the Location menu's order (A to Z), separated by a comma and a space, for example "Locations: Main Shop, North Bay". Under it, only when set: "Past due: " and the ticked ranges in menu order (for example "Past due: 31-60 days, 91+ days"), then, for one ticked customer or vendor, "Customer: " or "Vendor: " and the name, and for several, "Customers: " or "Vendors: " and the names in the order they were ticked."
- **S3-R6**: "The Location column appears in the export under the same rule as on screen (Story 6)."
- **S3-R7**: "The A/R and A/P Summary PDF and CSV head the last bucket column "91 And Over", exactly as the screen shows it; in the PDF the heading stays on one line."
- **S3-R8**: "The CSV starts with the lines of S3-R5, each in its own row, in this order: As of, Locations, then Past due and the customer or vendor line only when set. The column heading row follows directly, then one flat table: one row per customer or vendor (Summaries) or per document (Detail, Collection, Unpaid Invoices), with the aging bucket, customer or vendor in its own column; no group header or subtotal rows; one Totals row at the bottom. Money is a plain number with two decimals, and an empty bucket is a blank cell; dates are YYYY-MM-DD."
- **S3-R8a**: "On A/R Collection, every CSV row also carries the customer's "Phone" and, for users with 'View and Manage AP/AR Data', "Credit term", in columns right after "Customer"; the PDF prints them on each customer's line (Story 9)."
- **S3-R8b**: "The CSV column headings are the on-screen headings, letter for letter (S2-R4a): "Customer Full Name" on A/R Detail, "Customer" on A/R Collection, "Vendor Name" on the A/P reports. The bucket column on A/R Detail and A/P Detail is headed "Aging Bucket", and its values are the bucket names shown on screen."
- **S3-R9**: "The row count for the export limits is the number of customer or vendor rows (Summaries) or document rows (the other four), not counting headers, subtotals or the Totals row."
- **S4-N1**: "Changing the as-of date, the customer or vendor filter, the Past due filter or the Location filter collapses every group, as changing the date or filter does today."
- **S4-N2**: "A/R Summary and A/P Summary have no groups, so they have no Collapse all button."
- **S4-R1**: "The table's top-left header cell holds one chevron button. While any group is expanded it points down, its tooltip (above the button) reads "Collapse all", and a click collapses every group. When every group is collapsed it points right, its tooltip reads "Expand all", and a click expands every group."
- **S4-R2**: "After Expand all, groups that arrive later as the user scrolls also arrive expanded."
- **S4-R3**: "Clicking the button never sorts the column under it."
- **S4-R4**: "On A/R Detail and A/P Detail, expanding a bucket (by hand or with Expand all) shows its first 100 lines, then loads more as the user scrolls. This is as today on A/P Detail and new on A/R Detail, which today shows a whole bucket at once."
- **S4-R5**: "Each group's own expand toggle uses the same chevron: pointing down when open, right when closed. Behavior is unchanged."
- **S4-R6**: "While the user scrolls through an expanded group, that group's row (the bucket label, customer or vendor) stays pinned just under the column headings. Only one group row is held at a time: when the next group's row reaches it, the next row pushes it up and off and takes its place. Scrolling back reverses this."
- **S4-R7**: "When the table scrolls sideways, each group row's label stays visible at the left."
- **S4-R8**: "Re-sorting and paging keep every group's expanded or collapsed state."
- **S4-R9**: "Expanded state is not remembered between visits."
- **S5-E1**: "If a location is chosen and the user then picks a customer or vendor with nothing open there, the report is empty and the Location chip stays visible so the selection can be cleared."
- **S5-E2**: "Switching location in the top right of ShopView while on a report reloads it, as on every other report: the user stays on the report with the same filters and the same rows; the sort returns to the opening order (Story 1), every group collapses and the page returns to the top; which documents link, prompt or show as plain text updates (Story 7)."
- **S5-N1**: "If the organization's open documents sit at only one location and it is ticked, the Location chip is not shown."
- **S5-N2**: "A location in a link that is not in the report's Location list (S5-R3), or is not a valid location at all, is ignored: the report loads, the chip reads as if it were not there, and the Location column follows the remaining choice."
- **S5-N3**: "A Past due value in a link that the chip does not offer (for example 5, or "any") is read as Any."
- **S5-R1**: "Each report's filter row is the native report filter row of the newer reports (Inventory Value, Sales By Customer), in this order on all six reports: the date chip ("As of: 10/15/2026"), the customer chip on the A/R reports or the vendor chip on the A/P reports, the Past due chip, then the Location chip."
- **S5-R10**: "The ticked locations are kept in the page address while fewer than all are ticked, so browser Back, a reload and a shared link restore them; a link listing several locations separated by commas is read as those locations. "No locations" is not kept in the address: a reload or shared link opens on All locations, as on Sales By Customer. A link naming every location reads as All locations."
- **S5-R10a**: "Filter choices are not remembered between visits: opening a report from the Reports menu starts on today's as-of date, no customer or vendor, Past due Any, and All locations."
- **S5-R11**: "Clicking a customer or vendor on A/R Summary or A/P Summary opens A/R Collection or A/P Unpaid Invoices with just that customer or vendor ticked, the same as-of date, the same ticked Past due ranges and the same ticked locations."
- **S5-R12**: "The filter does not change who can see what: everyone who can open these reports sees every location's data, as today."
- **S5-R13**: "The customer chip (A/R) and vendor chip (A/P) and their open menus are the native ones: Customer exactly as Sales By Customer's Customer filter (person icon), Vendor exactly as Inventory Value's Vendor filter (truck icon, "Search vendors"; as there, the search box shows only when there are more than 8 vendors). The menu has a search box, an "All customers" or "All vendors" row, the native line "All customers are included. Pick one or more to narrow." (or vendors) while none is ticked, and the list; several can be ticked. The chip reads "Customer: All customers" or "Vendor: All vendors" in the resting (grey) style when none is ticked, the name when one is (shortened with "..." when long), and "Customer: N customers" or "Vendor: N vendors" when N are. The ticked customers or vendors narrow every row, group, total, page, sort and export; the Location list does not change (S5-R3)."
- **S5-R14**: "A customer or vendor ticked through a link, a reload or a Summary drill-down is named and ticked on the chip, even when it is not among the first results of the chip's search."
- **S5-R15**: "The Past due chip and its open menu are the same native checkbox dropdown as Customer and Location: label "Past due", an hourglass icon, an "Any" row, the native line "All ranges are included. Pick one or more to narrow." while none is ticked, then "1-30 days", "31-60 days", "61-90 days", "91+ days", in that order; several can be ticked. The chip reads "Past due: Any" in the resting (grey) style when none is ticked, the range when one is (for example "Past due: 91+ days"), and "Past due: N ranges" when N are. Ticking all four reads "Past due: 4 ranges", not Any, because Any also keeps invoices and bills that are not due yet. The ranges are the reports' own aging buckets: 1 to 30, 31 to 60, 61 to 90 and more than 90 days past the due date on the as-of date."
- **S5-R16**: "With ranges ticked, A/R Detail, A/R Collection, A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices keep only the invoices and bills whose days past due on the as-of date fall in a ticked range, and always keep credit memos, deposits and vendor credits."
- **S5-R17**: "With ranges ticked, A/R Summary and A/P Summary list only the customers or vendors with some open invoice or bill amount in a ticked range; their rows show their full balances in every bucket."
- **S5-R18**: "The Totals row always adds up exactly the rows the filters leave (customer or vendor, Past due, Location), on screen on every page, in the PDF and in the CSV. On A/R Collection and A/P Unpaid Invoices this fixes today's Totals row, which ignores the customer or vendor filter."
- **S5-R18a**: "For the same as-of date, customer or vendor and Location, with Past due on Any, A/R Summary's Totals equal A/R Detail's Open Balance Totals bucket by bucket, and A/P Summary's equal A/P Detail's. The Detail reports' Amount column holds original amounts and is not compared. With a Past due filter they can differ: a Summary keeps the whole row of a customer or vendor that qualifies (Current and credits included), while a Detail report lists only the invoices or bills that qualify, plus every credit."
- **S5-R18b**: "The figures are read fresh on every load: reloading the report shows a payment recorded a moment before. Going away and back inside the app within 30 seconds, without reloading, can show the figures from the earlier visit, as on every screen."
- **S5-R19**: "The ticked customers or vendors and the ticked Past due ranges are kept in the page address like Location (S5-R10)."
- **S5-R2**: "The Location chip and its open menu are the native ones, exactly as on Sales By Customer, Inventory Value and Work In Progress: label "Location" with the location pin icon; the menu shows a "Select all" row, a "{N} selected" count, the locations with the ticked ones first, a search box with the placeholder "Search locations" once there are more than 8 locations, and "Clear selection"."
- **S5-R3**: "The list offers every location at which the organization has open documents in this report on the as-of date, whatever location the user is signed into and whatever locations they are enrolled in. It is not narrowed by the Location filter, the customer or vendor filter, or the Past due filter; it changes only with the as-of date."
- **S5-R4**: "Each location is named exactly as the location switcher in the top right of ShopView names it, and the Location column uses the same name."
- **S5-R5**: "The filter opens with every location in the list ticked (All locations), so an untouched report shows exactly today's organization-wide rows and totals. "Select all" ticks every location again."
- **S5-R6**: "The chip reads "Location: All locations" when every location is ticked, the location's name when one is ticked (shortened with "..." when long), "Location: N locations" when N are ticked, and "Location: No locations" when none is."
- **S5-R6a**: "With no location ticked ("Clear selection" or unticking every row), the report shows no rows: the native empty state (S8-R5) with "Clear all filters", no totals and no Location column. An export from that state downloads nothing and shows "Empty export", "Export didn't yield any results", as on Sales By Customer."
- **S5-R7**: "Choosing locations narrows every row, group, subtotal, total, page count, sort and export to the open documents at those locations. A row belongs to the location of its own document. On A/R: the invoice, credit memo or deposit. On A/P: a bill belongs to the location of the delivery it was received on; a vendor credit from a vendor return belongs to that return's location; any other vendor credit keeps its vendor's location, as today. That is the location the Location column shows."
- **S5-R8**: "On the Summaries, a customer's or vendor's row adds up only their open documents at the chosen locations; a customer or vendor with nothing open there is not listed."
- **S5-R9**: "The chip is shown whenever the report's list (S5-R3) has two or more locations, and always while fewer than all are ticked, so the choice can always be undone."
- **S6-N1**: "With one location chosen, the column is hidden on screen and in the export."
- **S6-R1**: "All six reports have a column labelled exactly "Location". This renames A/R Detail's and A/R Collection's "Location full name" and A/P Detail's and A/P Unpaid Invoices' "Location Name", and adds the column to A/R Summary and A/P Summary."
- **S6-R2**: "The column is shown when two or more locations are ticked (All locations counts when the report's list has two or more) and hidden when one or none is ticked."
- **S6-R3**: "It sits right after the Date column, before the money columns; on A/R Summary and A/P Summary, which have no Date column, right after the customer or vendor name."
- **S6-R4**: "On A/R Summary and A/P Summary a row shows the location's name when all of that customer's or vendor's open documents at the chosen locations are at one location, otherwise "Multiple"."
- **S6-R5**: "The column sorts A to Z on its first click; "Multiple" sorts as text."
- **S7-E1**: "The location switcher in the top right of ShopView is not changed."
- **S7-N1**: "A document at a location the user is not enrolled in shows as plain text."
- **S7-N2**: "A document the user lacks the permission for shows as plain text."
- **S7-R1**: "A document number is a link only when the user has the permission to open it and can open it in its own location; otherwise it shows as plain text."
- **S7-R10**: "A/R credit memos and deposits show as plain text: they have no page of their own."
- **S7-R2**: "An invoice or part sale at the user's current location opens its finance page, as today."
- **S7-R3**: "An invoice or part sale at another location the user is enrolled in shows the existing location-switch prompt on the report, before leaving it: title "Switch location?", message "This work order is in {location}. Switch your active location to view it?" (for a part sale: "This part sale is in {location}. Switch your active location to view it?"), buttons "Cancel" on the left and "Switch location" on the right, and a close × at the top right."
- **S7-R4**: ""Switch location" changes the active location to that location and opens the document."
- **S7-R5**: ""Cancel" or the × closes the prompt; the user stays on the report exactly where they were: same page address, filters, sort, expanded groups and scroll position."
- **S7-R6**: "A bill at the user's current location opens its delivery, as today. A bill at another location shows as plain text, because the bill page cannot switch location."
- **S7-R7**: "Links use the native report link style (the same blue as Work In Progress, semi-bold, no underline, underlined only on keyboard focus), readable in light and dark theme."
- **S7-R8**: "Opening a link in a new tab opens the document page, which asks to switch location itself."
- **S7-R9**: "On A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices, a vendor credit's number links to the vendor return it came from, under the same rules and permissions as a bill (S7-R6). A vendor credit that did not come from a vendor return shows as plain text."
- **S8-N1**: "The descriptions appear on screen only, not in the PDF or CSV."
- **S8-R1**: "Each report has the native report info icon (the one Work In Progress and Technician Utilization use) directly right of its title. Hovering, keyboard focus or a tap shows the report's description in a tooltip; moving away or Escape closes it. The descriptions:"
- **S8-R2**: "The title, its info icon and the Export menu stay on one line at 1440 pixels wide; the filter row sits below them, positioned as on the native reports."
- **S8-R3**: "The headline column (Open Balance; Total on the Summaries) is pinned to the right edge and bold on the header, every row, every expanded row and the Totals row, as on Sales By Customer. At 1440 pixels wide every column of all six reports is visible without scrolling sideways: the customer or vendor name and Transaction Type wrap long values onto a second line, Location wraps only on A/P Detail (nine columns), column headings never wrap, and money, date and number columns never wrap. Narrower than 1440 pixels, the table scrolls sideways under the pinned column."
- **S8-R4**: "The Totals row uses the native totals row style of the newer reports."
- **S8-R5**: "While a report loads it shows the native table progress bar. When the filters leave no rows, it shows the native empty state: "No record matches these filters" and "Try changing filters to widen your results.", with a "Clear all filters" button when a customer or vendor, Past due or Location is chosen; that button keeps the as-of date and returns the customer or vendor, Past due and Location to their defaults (every location ticked)."
- **S8-R6**: "If a report fails to load, it shows "Something went wrong loading this report. Please try again." in place of the rows, with the standard error message."
- **S9-N1**: "A customer with no phone shows no "Phone:" label, on screen or in the PDF."
- **S9-N2**: "A user without 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' sees no credit term on screen, none in the PDF, and no Credit term column in the CSV."
- **S9-R1**: "On A/R Collection, each customer's group row shows, after the customer's name, "Phone: " and the customer's phone number as the customer card shows it."
- **S9-R2**: "For users with the 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' switch, the group row also shows "Credit term: " and the customer's credit term as the customer card shows it ("COD" when none is set)."
- **S9-R3**: "The PDF prints the same phone and credit term on each customer's line; the CSV carries them in columns (S3-R8a)."
- **ASSUME-13540** (Assumptions): "A shop's A/P Detail can carry well over 10,000 open lines (the QA organization has 13,540 on one as-of date and 437 vendors); A/R Summary in the same organization has 46 customers."
- **EH** (Story 3, Error Handling): "If an export fails for any other reason (a server error or a lost connection), nothing downloads and one error message shows: "We couldn't export this report. Please try again." This message is on these six reports only; the other reports keep theirs."
- **FORM** (Form factor): "Form factor: Desktop only by rule: these are detailed reports."
- **GOAL-ACCESS** (Goals): "Nobody gains or loses access to any data, and no setup is needed."
- **KD-DRILL** (Key Decisions, drill-down): "Drill-down keeps the report's scope. Clicking a customer or vendor on a Summary opens Collection or Unpaid Invoices with the same as-of date and the same chosen locations. Basis: d-drill-in-inherits-scope."
- **KD-ORG** (Key Decisions, organization-wide): "The reports stay organization-wide, by decision. Everyone with access sees every location's data, as today; the Location filter only narrows. Rationale: no permission changes in this release (decided 2026-10-15). Rejected: restricting the reports to the locations a user is enrolled in, which would be its own announced permissions change later."
- **KD-PERM** (Key Decisions, Permissions): "Permissions. Viewing all six reports, and exporting them, is governed by 'Reports → Page Access', unchanged. No new permission is introduced and no existing permission changes. Opening a document from a report uses the permission that already opens that document (see Story 7). The credit term on the A/R Collection call list follows the customer card's rule: only users with the 'View and Manage AP/AR Data' switch see it, on screen, in the PDF and in the CSV (Story 9). Amounts show to everyone who can open the reports, as today and as on Sales By Customer and Inventory Value; 'See Financial Data' does not hide them."
- **KD-REUSE** (Key Decisions, reuse rule): "The reuse rule. Every control on these reports reuses an existing ShopView pattern: the native filter chips, the native totals row and pinned headline column of the newer reports, the existing location-switch prompt, the design system's chevron icon. Nothing new is drawn. Basis: d-reuse-existing-pattern."
- **KD-ROW** (Key Decisions, row location): "A row belongs to the location of its own open document (the invoice, credit memo or deposit; the bill or vendor credit), the same location the Location column shows. Rationale: the aging reports list open documents, not payments, so a partly paid invoice keeps its own location and the filter never contradicts the column. Rejected: splitting a balance by where each payment was taken."
- **OOS-1** (Out of Scope): "Background exports for very large reports ("we'll email you when it's ready"), as Sage Intacct does. In this release, over the export limits the user is told to choose an earlier as-of date. Background exports come as a later phase of this epic (SV-11445): over the limit, the export is prepared in the background and the user gets an in-app notification with a download link. That phase is designed and specified before it is planned."
- **OOS-2** (Out of Scope): "Restricting these reports by the user's location enrollment. The reports stay organization-wide for everyone who can open them today; the Location filter only narrows. Any enrollment-based restriction would be its own announced permissions change."
- **OOS-3** (Out of Scope): "Renaming columns other than Location. Only the capitals change (S2-R4a): A/R keeps "ID" and "Customer Full Name", A/P keeps "No." and "Vendor Name"."
- **OOS-4** (Out of Scope): "Other improvements found in research, each a separate ticket on SV-11315: a credit dated on the as-of date landing in 1-30 instead of Current (SV-11441, checked against QuickBooks first); configurable aging periods (SV-11442); send a statement from the report (SV-11443); salesperson and Parts vs Service filters (SV-11444); last payment date and credit limit (SV-11446); a collections notes log (SV-11447)."
- **OOS-5** (Out of Scope): "Email and a primary contact on the A/R Collection call list. A customer has no primary contact in ShopView today; the call list shows the phone and credit term only."
- **OOS-6** (Out of Scope): "Links for A/R credit memos and deposits. They have no page of their own to open, so they stay plain text."
- **OOS-7** (Out of Scope): "A/P print from a separate print button. A/P printing is delivered through the Export menu's PDF (SV-6163)."
- **OOS-8** (Out of Scope): "The location switcher in the top right of ShopView. It is not changed. Switching location while on one of these reports reloads it, as on every other report: the filters and the rows stay, because the reports are organization-wide; the sort returns to the opening order, groups collapse and the page returns to the top; which documents link, prompt or show as plain text updates (Story 7)."
- **OOS-9** (Out of Scope): "A phone or tablet layout. Narrower than 1024 pixels, the reports show the native mobile filter row and the table scrolls sideways; nothing else is designed for small screens in this release."
- **UFS-1** (User Feedback Summary): "PDF export over 2,000 rows | "This report is too large for PDF. Export it as CSV, or narrow the filters, then try again." | Error toast, as on the other reports; nothing downloads"
- **UFS-10** (User Feedback Summary): "Report fails to load | "Something went wrong loading this report. Please try again." | In place of the rows, plus the standard error toast"
- **UFS-2** (User Feedback Summary): "CSV export over 50,000 rows | "This report is too large to export. Choose an earlier as-of date, then try again." | Error toast, as on the other reports; nothing downloads"
- **UFS-3** (User Feedback Summary): "Click an invoice at another enrolled location | Title "Switch location?", message "This work order is in {location}. Switch your active location to view it?" | Dialog on the report; "Switch location" switches and opens, "Cancel" stays"
- **UFS-4** (User Feedback Summary): "Click a part sale at another enrolled location | "This part sale is in {location}. Switch your active location to view it?" | Same dialog"
- **UFS-5** (User Feedback Summary): "Collapse all / Expand all button | "Collapse all" / "Expand all" | Tooltip above the button"
- **UFS-6** (User Feedback Summary): "Export succeeds | "Success", "Data exported successfully." | Success toast, as on the other reports"
- **UFS-7** (User Feedback Summary): "Export with no rows | "Empty export", "Export didn't yield any results" | Warning toast; nothing downloads"
- **UFS-8** (User Feedback Summary): "Export fails for any other reason | "We couldn't export this report. Please try again." | Error toast; nothing downloads; these six reports only"
- **UFS-9** (User Feedback Summary): "Filters leave no rows | "No record matches these filters", "Try changing filters to widen your results." | Native empty state with "Clear all filters""
- **WC-1** (What changes for existing users): "A/R reports no longer come out in an unpredictable order; A/R Summary and A/R Collection no longer repeat or skip customers between pages."
- **WC-10** (What changes for existing users): "Which rows and amounts each user can see is unchanged: an untouched report shows exactly today's organization-wide numbers (apart from the Totals rows noted above), and the reports show money exactly as they do today."
- **WC-11** (What changes for existing users): "The A/R column headings change to Title Case, as on the A/P reports and the other reports: "Transaction Type", "Customer Full Name", "Due Date", "Past Due", "Open Balance"."
- **WC-2** (What changes for existing users): "A/P Detail opens with rows in vendor name order inside each bucket (today: most overdue first)."
- **WC-3** (What changes for existing users): "A/R's Print button becomes the Export menu; its PDF prints the on-screen order and the as-of date the user picked (today it can print the next day's date)."
- **WC-4** (What changes for existing users): "The Totals row on A/R Collection and A/P Unpaid Invoices now follows the customer or vendor filter; today it ignores it, on production too."
- **WC-5** (What changes for existing users): "The A/R Summary Totals row now adds up exactly the customer rows above it. Today it also counts overpaid invoices (an invoice paid for more than its amount), which the rows don't, so for those customers the Totals don't match the rows. Production has 16 such invoices across 13 customer accounts; their A/R Summary Totals will differ slightly from today. The rows themselves don't change."
- **WC-6** (What changes for existing users): "Clicking a row no longer opens it. The document number, or the customer or vendor name on the Summaries, is the link, as on the newer reports (Story 7)."
- **WC-7** (What changes for existing users): "The date filter is labelled "As of" (was "Date"). On A/R Collection and A/P Unpaid Invoices the Customer or Vendor chip now comes after it."
- **WC-8** (What changes for existing users): "Re-sorting A/R Collection or A/P Unpaid Invoices no longer collapses the open groups."
- **WC-9** (What changes for existing users): "The A/R column "Location full name" and the A/P column "Location Name" are renamed "Location"."
- **S2-R1 ARS** (S2-R1, A/R Summary and A/P Summary columns): "A/R Summary and A/P Summary: the name column, Location (when shown), Current, 1-30 Days, 31-60 Days, 61-90 Days, 91 And Over, Total."
- **S2-R1 ARD** (S2-R1, A/R Detail columns): "A/R Detail: Date, Location (when shown), Transaction Type, ID, Customer Full Name, Due Date, Amount, Open Balance."
- **S2-R1 ARC** (S2-R1, A/R Collection columns): "A/R Collection: Date, Location (when shown), Transaction Type, ID, Due Date, Past Due, Amount, Open Balance."
- **S2-R1 APD** (S2-R1, A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices columns): "A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices: Date, Location (when shown), Transaction Type, No., Vendor Name, Due Date, Past Due, Amount, Open Balance."
- **S8-R1 ARS** (S8-R1, A/R Summary description): "A/R Summary: "Each customer’s unpaid balance as of the selected date, after unused credit memos and deposits, with a breakdown of overdue amounts.""
- **S8-R1 ARD** (S8-R1, A/R Detail description): "A/R Detail: "Each customer’s unpaid invoices, unused credit memos and deposits as of the selected date, grouped by how overdue they are.""
- **S8-R1 ARC** (S8-R1, A/R Collection description): "A/R Collection: "Overdue invoices, unused credit memos and deposits for each customer to help you follow up on payments. Excludes invoices that aren’t due yet.""
- **S8-R1 APS** (S8-R1, A/P Summary description): "A/P Summary: "Your unpaid balance with each vendor as of the selected date, after unused vendor credits, with a breakdown of overdue amounts.""
- **S8-R1 APD** (S8-R1, A/P Detail description): "A/P Detail: "Unpaid bills and unused vendor credits as of the selected date, grouped by how overdue they are.""
- **S8-R1 APU** (S8-R1, A/P Unpaid Invoices description): "A/P Unpaid Invoices: "Unpaid bills and unused vendor credits grouped by vendor, showing each bill’s due date and days overdue.""
- **S2-R1 APS** (S2-R1, A/R Summary and A/P Summary columns): "A/R Summary and A/P Summary: the name column, Location (when shown), Current, 1-30 Days, 31-60 Days, 61-90 Days, 91 And Over, Total."
- **S2-R1 APU** (S2-R1, A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices columns): "A/P Detail and A/P Unpaid Invoices: Date, Location (when shown), Transaction Type, No., Vendor Name, Due Date, Past Due, Amount, Open Balance."
- **Jira SV-11597** (developer verify task): "A work order opened from a link at another location still asks to switch, and Cancel still returns to Work Orders with its message."
- **Jira SV-11597** (developer verify task): "Inventory Value, Sales By Customer and Work In Progress: filters, links shared from the address bar, and CSV/PDF exports behave exactly as before."
- **Key Decisions, name order**: "Names sort the way QuickBooks sorts them: plain character order, capitals ignored, accented letters with their base letter."
- **Story 7 Prerequisites**: "A bill opens for a user with 'Vendor Order Management → View' or 'Vendor Order Management → Create & Edit'."
- **Story 7 Prerequisites**: "A customer name on A/R Summary links to A/R Collection for a user with 'Customers → View', as today."
- **Story 7 Prerequisites**: "A vendor name on A/P Summary links to A/P Unpaid Invoices for everyone who can open the reports, as today."
- **Story 7 Prerequisites**: "An invoice or part sale opens for a user with 'Invoicing & Payments → View' and 'Work Orders → View' (work orders) or 'Part Sales → View' (part sales)."
- **Tech plan §1 Clarifications, Q2 A/P row location**: "A vendor account is per (vendor, location) on every write path (delivery, manual credit, return credit), so vendor_account.workplace_id already is the document's location."
- **Tech plan §1 NFR-005**: "FE sends one request per settled filter change: chip→query path debounced (~400 ms), superseded requests aborted, opening fetch gated until URL state is reconciled."
- **Tech plan §3 D13**: "Equal to S5-R7's per-document rule by construction (every write path finds or creates the (vendor, location) account); prod 0 mismatches."
- **Tech plan §3 D16**: "Past due tokens 1-30, 31-60, 61-90, 91-plus on the wire and in the URL"
- **Tech plan §3 D20**: "The native date chip already caps at today (maxDate); label and data always agree."
- **Tech plan §3 D21**: "Pinned group row = scroll-driven overlay (ReportPinnedGroupRow + useReportPinnedGroup) showing the group row's chevron and full label — on A/R Collection including "Phone:" and "Credit term:" (S9-R1/R2) — but no money cells (the group's subtotals stay in its own row)"
- **Tech plan §3 D28**: "Empty-export detection reads the report's server count, never loaded rows: Summaries pagination.rowsNumber; Collection/Unpaid totals.documentCount; Detail totals.rowsNumber from the groups query"
- **Tech plan §3 D28, rationale**: "On Detail reports with every bucket collapsed no rows are loaded, so a loaded-rows check would wrongly say "Empty export" (S3-R10)."
- **Tech plan §3 D29**: "Unknown or unresolvable customer/vendor/location ids in a link are dropped by the FE during the opening reconcile (resolve endpoint / option list) before any request, so the BE 422 is never reached from the UI"
- **Tech plan §3 D29, rationale**: "Mirrors S5-N2 for customers/vendors (a deleted or merged customer in an old link must not break the report)."
- **Tech plan §3 D30**: "URL keys asOfDate, customerIds / vendorIds, pastDue, locationIds (repeated keys written, comma lists also read); legacy customerId / vendorId read once as aliases."
- **Tech plan §3 D30, tick order**: "Selections keep tick order end to end (chip state → URL → export params → resolve endpoint returns request order); only pastDue is normalized to menu order."
- **Tech plan §3 D31**: "Customer search (ar-aging/customers) searches all of the organization's customers, as Sales By Customer's chip does; vendor list = all of the organization's vendors, as Inventory Value's chip does"
- **Tech plan §3 D4**: "Name order name COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci, then LOWER(name) COLLATE utf8mb4_bin, then the entity id"
- **Tech plan §3 D9**: "As-of label via the existing AsOfAnchorResolver (api/src/Reporting/Shared/Application/AsOf/), wrapped by a thin AgingAsOfResolver that supplies the workplace timezone and produces both formats: PDF "As of Oct 15, 2026", CSV "As of: Oct 15, 2026" (M j, Y)"
- **Tech plan §3 D9, rationale**: "Fixes the A/R PDF printing the next day's date (S3-R4)."
- **Tech plan §5 API, new endpoints**: "ar-aging/customers (search, limit ≤ 50), ap-aging/vendors (org vendor list)"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 1, frontend**: "'stay' cancel closes the dialog with no toast/no push (S7-R5)"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 1, frontend**: "opt-in fullSelectionNarrows flag: no "All <noun>" for a full selection, select-all row ticked only when empty ("Past due: 4 ranges", S5-R15)"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 1, unit tests**: "FE: 5 rapid ticks → 1 fetch; superseded signal aborted;"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 1, unit tests**: "invalid location ignored / all-invalid → All; pastDue=5 → Any;"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 2, frontend**: "Until Phase 3 the link keeps today's target query (asOfDate, customerId, customerName), because the legacy Collection page reads only those;"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 3, frontend**: "switch the name link to the full drill-down scope (S5-R11): asOfDate, customerIds/vendorIds = [id], pastDue, locationIds (only when not All)"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 3, frontend**: "whole-row click removed (onRowClick, canNavigate, cursor-pointer, text-blue)"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 3, verification**: "Expand all + scroll loads later groups expanded;"
- **Tech plan §6 Phase 4, frontend**: "sort resets expanded buckets to page 1 but keeps them expanded"
- **Tech plan §7 E2E backlog**: "PDF generator not configured on every environment; CSV covers wiring"
- **Tech plan §7 E2E, regression re-runs**: "Regression re-runs (Keep, no edits expected) when Phase 1 lands, because it changes the shared filter kit, URL sync, useReportTableQuery and the location resolver:"
- **Tech plan §7 Manual testing checklist**: "3. Fling-scroll a long expanded group: pinned row pushes up without overlapping header or totals."
- **Tech plan §9 Known risks**: "PDF generation: WeasyPrint HTTP (600 s client timeout) pins an FPM worker; the 2,000-row cap bounds it (~30 s)."
- **Tech plan §9 Performance**: "Name order is UCA order (utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci): every PRD example holds, but punctuation sorts below digits and punctuation marks order among themselves differently from ASCII (e.g. - before #)."
- **Tech plan §9 Security**: "CSV formula-injection hardening via CsvAttachmentResponseBuilder (system numeric columns masked as safe)."

## 10. VIU status
- Every case and recipe: UNVERIFIED — the build is not yet available. The design was read and every case re-checked against it on 22 Oct 2026; the tech plan was read and every case re-checked against it the same day; every case was re-checked against PRD version 9 and Chris Ward's answers 1 to 16 on 26 Oct 2026.

## 11. Questions (Chris Ward's sheet, questions 1 to 16): all answered 23 Oct 2026, read 26 Oct 2026; PRD version 9 carries the answers
- Q1: 1. (a) "Emile's Towing" first. Accents fold to their base letter (S1-R3), so the two names tie, and S1-R4 breaks the tie by the plain lower-cased name, where the plain E comes first. I'll add this example to S1-R4.
- Q2: 2. (a) Smallest number first; the second click gives largest first.
- Q3: 3. (a) Tied rows stay smallest document number first in both directions. Only the sorted column reverses.
- Q4: 4. (c) One style on all six reports: Title Case, like the other reports (Sales By Customer, Inventory Value, Parts Velocity): "Open Balance", "Due Date", "Past Due", "Transaction Type", "Customer Full Name". Screen, PDF and CSV. Only the A/R headings change; I'll add it under "What changes for existing users".
- Q5: 5. (b) The next 30 load as the user scrolls down, as today. No page buttons. I'll reword S1-R8.
- Q6: 6. (a) Yes, the sort stays when Past due changes. I'll add Past due to S2-R7.
